## 🔤 A (Extra)

**ABATE** *(verb)* | কমানো, থামানো, দুর্বল করা
To become less intense, severe, or widespread; to reduce or lessen.
✅ reduce, lessen, decrease, diminish, subside, moderate, ease, wane
❌ increase, intensify, worsen, grow, strengthen, escalate, heighten
📝 The storm began to **abate** after midnight, and calm slowly returned.

**ABERRANT** *(adjective)* | স্বাভাবিকতার বাইরে, অস্বাভাবিক, বিপথগামী
Departing from an accepted standard; deviating from what is normal.
✅ abnormal, deviant, unusual, atypical, irregular, anomalous, strange
❌ normal, typical, standard, conventional, expected, regular, orthodox
📝 The **aberrant** results in the experiment prompted a full re-examination.

**ABJURE** *(verb)* | শপথ করে ত্যাগ করা, প্রকাশ্যে অস্বীকার করা
To formally reject or disavow a formerly held belief; to renounce.
✅ renounce, reject, abandon, disavow, retract, recant, forswear
❌ embrace, uphold, affirm, support, maintain, defend, hold on to
📝 Under pressure, he chose to **abjure** the radical ideas he had once championed.

**ACCOST** *(verb)* | সরাসরি এগিয়ে গিয়ে কথা বলা
To approach and address someone boldly or aggressively; to confront.
✅ approach, confront, address, stop, waylay, buttonhole, intercept
❌ avoid, ignore, pass by, flee from, turn away, retreat from
📝 A stranger **accosted** him on the street and demanded directions to the station.

**ADMONISH** *(verb)* | সতর্ক করা, মৃদু ভর্ৎসনা করা
To warn or reprimand someone firmly but not harshly; to advise seriously.
✅ warn, caution, rebuke, counsel, reprove, chide, urge, advise
❌ praise, commend, reward, encourage, support, congratulate, approve
📝 The coach **admonished** the young player for losing focus in training.

**AFFECTATION** *(noun)* | ভান করা, কৃত্রিম আচরণ, নাটকীয়তা
Behaviour, speech, or writing that is artificial and designed to impress.
✅ pretence, artificiality, posturing, mannerism, show, insincerity
❌ naturalness, sincerity, authenticity, genuineness, simplicity, openness
📝 His **affectation** of speaking with a foreign accent annoyed his colleagues.

**AGGREGATE** *(noun/verb)* | সমষ্টি, মোট, একত্রিত করা
A whole formed by combining several separate elements; total.
✅ total, sum, whole, collection, combination, accumulated, combined
❌ individual, separate, part, component, single, divided, fragmented
📝 The **aggregate** score across all three rounds determined the final winner.

**AGGRIEVE** *(verb)* | অপমান করা, ক্ষতি করা, কষ্ট দেওয়া
To feel resentment at unfair treatment; to distress or offend someone.
✅ offend, wrong, harm, distress, injure, hurt, upset, trouble
❌ please, satisfy, help, benefit, comfort, soothe, reward, honour
📝 The workers felt deeply **aggrieved** by the unexpected pay cuts.

**AMORPHOUS** *(adjective)* | আকারহীন, অনির্দিষ্ট আকারের, অস্পষ্ট
Without a clearly defined shape or form; vague and hard to classify.
✅ shapeless, formless, vague, undefined, indistinct, unstructured
❌ defined, clear, structured, shaped, organized, formed, distinct
📝 The plan remained **amorphous** — no one could agree on a clear direction.

**ANATHEMA** *(noun)* | অভিশাপ, ঘৃণিত বস্তু বা ব্যক্তি, নিষিদ্ধ
Something or someone strongly disliked or rejected; a formal curse.
✅ abomination, curse, taboo, bane, hatred, object of loathing
❌ blessing, favourite, beloved object, treasure, admiration
📝 Corruption was absolute **anathema** to everything she had built her career on.

**ANTITHESIS** *(noun)* | বিপরীত, বৈপরীত্য, সম্পূর্ণ উল্টো
A person or thing that is the direct opposite of someone or something else.
✅ opposite, contrast, reverse, negation, contrary, counterpart
❌ similarity, parallel, likeness, match, equivalent, same
📝 Her quiet diplomacy was the complete **antithesis** of his aggressive style.

**APATHY** *(noun)* | উদাসীনতা, নিরুদ্বেগ, আগ্রহহীনতা
Lack of interest, enthusiasm, or concern; emotional indifference.
✅ indifference, disinterest, passivity, lethargy, unconcern, detachment
❌ enthusiasm, passion, concern, interest, energy, eagerness, care
📝 Voter **apathy** in the election led to the lowest turnout in decades.

**APPROPRIATE** *(adjective/verb)* | যথাযথ, উপযুক্ত; দখল করা বা নেওয়া
Suitable or proper in the circumstances; to take for one's own use.
✅ suitable, fitting, proper, apt, relevant, correct, applicable, right
❌ inappropriate, unsuitable, improper, wrong, irrelevant, unfit
📝 Her response was calm and entirely **appropriate** given the circumstances.

**ATONEMENT** *(noun)* | প্রায়শ্চিত্ত, ক্ষতিপূরণ, পাপমোচন
Reparation for a wrong or injury; making amends for past wrongdoing.
✅ reparation, amends, penance, reconciliation, expiation, restitution
❌ wrongdoing, harm, offence, sin, injury, crime, damage
📝 His public apology was seen as an act of genuine **atonement**.

**AUDACITY** *(noun)* | সাহসিকতা, দুঃসাহস, নির্লজ্জতা
The willingness to take bold risks; rude or disrespectful boldness.
✅ boldness, daring, nerve, impudence, bravery, cheek, courage
❌ timidity, cowardice, caution, modesty, fearfulness, restraint
📝 She had the **audacity** to challenge the senior director's plan publicly.

**AUSPICIOUS** *(adjective)* | শুভ, মঙ্গলময়, ভালো লক্ষণের
Giving a good indication of future success; favourable and promising.
✅ favourable, promising, propitious, encouraging, fortunate, lucky
❌ inauspicious, unfavourable, ominous, threatening, unlucky, bad
📝 The sunny weather was seen as an **auspicious** sign for the opening day.

**AVARICE** *(noun)* | লোভ, কৃপণতা, অর্থলিপ্সা
Extreme greed for wealth or material gain; miserliness; cupidity.
✅ greed, cupidity, acquisitiveness, covetousness, miserliness, meanness
❌ generosity, charity, altruism, selflessness, contentment, liberality
📝 His downfall was entirely the result of unchecked **avarice** and ambition.

**BABBLE** *(verb)* | বকবক করা, অর্থহীনভাবে কথা বলা
To talk rapidly and continuously in a foolish or confused way.
✅ prattle, chatter, ramble, jabber, gibber, talk nonsense, blather
❌ speak clearly, be concise, be silent, communicate effectively
📝 The nervous candidate began to **babble** under pressure during the interview.

**BEGUILE** *(verb)* | ভুলিয়ে দেওয়া, মুগ্ধ করা, সময় কাটানো
To charm or enchant someone, sometimes in a deceptive way; to trick.
✅ charm, enchant, captivate, trick, deceive, mislead, distract, amuse
❌ repel, bore, inform truthfully, reveal, warn, alert, disenchant
📝 She was completely **beguiled** by his charming manner and clever conversation.

**BELLICOSE** *(adjective)* | লড়াকু, যুদ্ধংদেহী, ঝগড়াটে
Demonstrating aggression and willingness to fight; warlike.
✅ aggressive, warlike, belligerent, combative, pugnacious, hostile
❌ peaceful, passive, peaceful, friendly, non-aggressive, cooperative
📝 His **bellicose** tone in negotiations made agreement almost impossible.

**BEMOAN** *(verb)* | আফসোস করা, বিলাপ করা, দুঃখ প্রকাশ করা
To express discontent or sorrow over something; to lament or deplore.
✅ lament, mourn, grieve over, deplore, regret, sorrow about, bewail
❌ celebrate, cheer, praise, rejoice, be happy about, welcome
📝 She often **bemoaned** the lack of funding for arts education in schools.

**BENEVOLENCE** *(noun)* | পরোপকারিতা, দয়া, উদারতা
The quality of being well meaning and kindly; generous good will.
✅ kindness, generosity, goodwill, charity, compassion, philanthropy
❌ selfishness, cruelty, malevolence, unkindness, avarice, hostility
📝 The billionaire's **benevolence** was evident in the scholarships he provided.

**BESEECH** *(verb)* | মিনতি করা, আকুলভাবে অনুরোধ করা
To ask someone urgently and fervently to do or provide something.
✅ implore, plead, beg, entreat, appeal, supplicate, urge, implore
❌ demand, order, command, instruct, dictate, insist, require
📝 She **beseeched** the committee to reconsider their decision one final time.

**BOMBASTIC** *(adjective)* | ফাঁকা বড়াই করা, অতিরঞ্জিত ভাষায় কথা বলা
High-sounding language with little meaning; inflated and empty language.
✅ pompous, verbose, grandiose, exaggerated, inflated, pretentious, wordy
❌ simple, plain, direct, modest, understated, sincere, clear
📝 The speech was full of **bombastic** phrases that impressed no one at all.

**CENSURE** *(verb/noun)* | নিন্দা করা, আনুষ্ঠানিক ভর্ৎসনা
An expression of strong disapproval; to criticize someone strongly.
✅ criticize, condemn, rebuke, reprimand, castigate, denounce, blame
❌ praise, commend, approve, support, endorse, congratulate, reward
📝 The council voted to **censure** the minister for misleading parliament.

**CIRCUMVENT** *(verb)* | চালাকিতে এড়ানো, কৌশলে পার হওয়া
To find a way around an obstacle; to overcome by clever methods.
✅ avoid, bypass, evade, outwit, sidestep, skirt, outmaneuver
❌ confront, face, comply with, obey, follow, adhere to, meet
📝 He found ways to **circumvent** the regulations without technically breaking them.

**CLAMOUR** *(noun/verb)* | জোরে দাবি করা, হৈচৈ, কোলাহল
A loud and confused noise; an insistent public demand.
✅ uproar, noise, commotion, outcry, shouting, din, racket, protest
❌ silence, calm, quiet, whisper, peace, tranquility, stillness
📝 There was a public **clamour** for the resignation of the corrupt official.

**DAUNT** *(verb)* | ভয় দেখানো, নিরুৎসাহিত করা
To make someone feel intimidated or apprehensive; to discourage.
✅ intimidate, frighten, discourage, unnerve, deter, frighten, put off
❌ encourage, embolden, inspire, motivate, reassure, support, strengthen
📝 The sheer scale of the task did not **daunt** her in the slightest.

**DEBUNK** *(verb)* | ভুল প্রমাণ করা, মিথ্যা দাবি খণ্ডন করা
To expose the falseness or hollowness of a myth, idea, or belief.
✅ disprove, expose, refute, discredit, contradict, challenge, reveal
❌ confirm, support, validate, prove, endorse, verify, strengthen
📝 The scientist worked to **debunk** the widespread misconception about vaccines.

**DECEPTIVE** *(adjective)* | প্রতারণামূলক, বিভ্রান্তিকর
Giving an appearance or impression different from the true one; misleading.
✅ misleading, dishonest, fraudulent, deceitful, false, illusory, tricky
❌ honest, truthful, transparent, accurate, genuine, reliable, direct
📝 The figures in the report were **deceptive** — they hid the true scale of the loss.

**DECRY** *(verb)* | প্রকাশ্যে নিন্দা করা, ছোট করা
To publicly denounce something; to express strong disapproval of.
✅ condemn, criticize, denounce, disparage, denigrate, deplore, attack
❌ praise, support, commend, approve, endorse, celebrate, promote
📝 Critics were quick to **decry** the government's decision as deeply unfair.

**DEFERENCE** *(noun)* | শ্রদ্ধাপূর্বক মেনে নেওয়া, সম্মান দেখানো
Humble submission and respect; yielding to the opinion of another.
✅ respect, submission, regard, reverence, compliance, yielding, honour
❌ disrespect, defiance, arrogance, contempt, insubordination, resistance
📝 She spoke with **deference** to the senior researcher whose expertise she respected.

**DENIGRATE** *(verb)* | নিন্দা করা, ছোট করা, হেয় প্রতিপন্ন করা
To criticize unfairly; to blacken someone's reputation; to belittle.
✅ belittle, defame, slander, criticize, disparage, insult, deprecate
❌ praise, commend, defend, celebrate, honour, respect, uphold
📝 Attempts to **denigrate** her work only made her more determined to succeed.

**DESIST** *(verb)* | বিরত হওয়া, থেমে যাওয়া, ছেড়ে দেওয়া
To stop doing something; to cease an action; to abstain from.
✅ stop, cease, halt, refrain, discontinue, give up, abstain, quit
❌ continue, persist, carry on, proceed, resume, maintain, press on
📝 He was ordered by the court to immediately **desist** from the activity.

**DETER** *(verb)* | বাধা দেওয়া, নিরুৎসাহিত করা, ঠেকানো
To discourage someone from doing something through doubt or fear.
✅ discourage, prevent, dissuade, inhibit, stop, block, hinder, check
❌ encourage, motivate, inspire, allow, support, promote, enable
📝 Harsh penalties are meant to **deter** others from committing similar crimes.

**DIFFUSE** *(verb/adjective)* | ছড়িয়ে দেওয়া, বিস্তার করা; বিক্ষিপ্ত
To spread over a wide area; to cause to become less concentrated.
✅ spread, scatter, disperse, circulate, disseminate, broadcast, distribute
❌ concentrate, gather, focus, collect, condense, centralize, limit
📝 Social media helps to **diffuse** both useful information and harmful rumours.

**DILATORY** *(adjective)* | ধীরগতি, বিলম্বকারী, টালবাহানার প্রবণ
Slow to act; causing or intended to cause delay; tending to procrastinate.
✅ slow, tardy, delaying, procrastinating, unhurried, dawdling, sluggish
❌ prompt, quick, fast, efficient, punctual, on time, decisive
📝 His **dilatory** approach to responding to emails frustrated the entire team.

**DISMAY** *(verb/noun)* | হতাশ করা, আতঙ্কিত করা; হতাশা
Consternation and distress caused by something unexpected; to horrify.
✅ alarm, distress, shock, horror, astonish, disappoint, unsettle
❌ reassure, comfort, delight, please, calm, encourage, impress
📝 She looked on with **dismay** as the carefully prepared event fell apart.

**DISPEL** *(verb)* | দূর করা, তাড়িয়ে দেওয়া
To make a doubt, feeling, or belief disappear; to drive away.
✅ eliminate, remove, dismiss, banish, scatter, drive away, clear
❌ create, encourage, increase, maintain, promote, reinforce, spread
📝 His warm welcome helped to **dispel** any doubts she had about coming.

**DISQUIET** *(noun/verb)* | উদ্বেগ, অস্থিরতা, অশান্তি
A feeling of worry or anxiety; to make someone feel anxious.
✅ anxiety, worry, unease, concern, nervousness, agitation, trouble
❌ calm, peace, reassurance, contentment, serenity, comfort, ease
📝 There was a growing **disquiet** among staff about the future of the company.

**DISSIPATE** *(verb)* | নষ্ট করা, ছড়িয়ে ফেলা, অপব্যয় করা
To cause to disappear or lose energy; to squander money or resources.
✅ scatter, disperse, waste, squander, fade, evaporate, disappear
❌ conserve, gather, save, maintain, build, accumulate, preserve
📝 The morning fog slowly began to **dissipate** as the sun climbed higher.

**DOGMATIC** *(adjective)* | গোঁড়া, নিজের মতামতে অনমনীয়
Inclined to lay down principles as undeniably true; rigidly opinionated.
✅ rigid, inflexible, opinionated, narrow-minded, dictatorial, stubborn
❌ open-minded, flexible, willing to change, receptive, adaptable
📝 His **dogmatic** approach to management prevented any innovation.

**DUPLICITOUS** *(adjective)* | ধোঁকাবাজ, দুই মুখো, প্রতারণামূলক
Deceitful; saying or doing different things to different people; two-faced.
✅ deceptive, dishonest, double-dealing, treacherous, two-faced, false
❌ honest, straightforward, sincere, genuine, transparent, trustworthy
📝 His **duplicitous** behaviour finally caught up with him when both stories crossed.

**ELICIT** *(verb)* | বের করে আনা, প্রকাশ করানো
To draw out a response, answer, or reaction from someone.
✅ draw out, evoke, extract, provoke, prompt, bring out, generate
❌ suppress, prevent, block, discourage, hide, conceal, stifle
📝 The professor's question was designed to **elicit** original thinking from students.

**EMBROIL** *(verb)* | জড়িত করা, বিবাদে টেনে আনা
To involve someone deeply in an argument, conflict, or difficult situation.
✅ involve, entangle, implicate, drag in, engulf, catch up, entrap
❌ free, disentangle, separate, remove, protect, extract, exclude
📝 He was determined not to be **embroiled** in someone else's personal dispute.

**EMPHATIC** *(adjective)* | জোরালো, স্পষ্ট ও নিশ্চিত, জোর দিয়ে বলা
Expressing something forcibly and clearly; definite and clear; strong.
✅ forceful, clear, strong, definite, positive, unambiguous, assertive
❌ weak, uncertain, vague, equivocal, hesitant, ambiguous, tentative
📝 She gave an **emphatic** "no" that left no doubt about her position.

**ENGROSS** *(verb)* | সম্পূর্ণ মনোযোগ নেওয়া, মগ্ন করা
To absorb all the attention or interest of someone; to occupy fully.
✅ absorb, occupy, captivate, engage, preoccupy, immerse, fascinate
❌ bore, distract, free, release, disinterest, repel, unoccupy
📝 She was so **engrossed** in her book that she did not hear the doorbell.

**ENNUI** *(noun)* | একঘেয়ে জীবনের বিরক্তি, অতৃপ্তি
A feeling of listlessness and dissatisfaction arising from lack of interest.
✅ boredom, listlessness, tedium, dissatisfaction, restlessness, apathy
❌ excitement, interest, enthusiasm, joy, engagement, passion, zeal
📝 A long spell of **ennui** set in after she finished the major project.

**EVASIVE** *(adjective)* | এড়িয়ে চলার প্রবণ, অস্পষ্ট উত্তর দেওয়া
Tending to avoid commitment or self-revelation; ambiguous or misleading.
✅ ambiguous, vague, elusive, slippery, non-committal, indirect, unclear
❌ direct, honest, straightforward, forthright, open, transparent, clear
📝 The minister's **evasive** answers during the press conference raised more questions.

**EXHAUSTIVE** *(adjective)* | পুঙ্খানুপুঙ্খ, সম্পূর্ণ, সব দিক কভার করা
Including or considering all elements; thorough and complete in scope.
✅ thorough, comprehensive, complete, detailed, extensive, all-inclusive
❌ incomplete, partial, superficial, cursory, brief, selective, narrow
📝 The **exhaustive** investigation left no stone unturned in finding the truth.

**EXQUISITE** *(adjective)* | অত্যন্ত সুন্দর, নিখুঁত, অসাধারণ মনোরম
Extremely beautiful and delicate; intensely felt; of special beauty.
✅ beautiful, elegant, perfect, delicate, refined, lovely, stunning
❌ ugly, crude, ordinary, coarse, unpleasant, rough, common
📝 The **exquisite** craftsmanship of the necklace left the jeweller speechless.

**EXTOL** *(verb)* | উচ্চ প্রশংসা করা, গুণকীর্তন করা
To praise enthusiastically; to speak very highly of someone or something.
✅ praise, laud, commend, celebrate, glorify, acclaim, exalt, honour
❌ criticize, condemn, disparage, belittle, dismiss, attack, denounce
📝 Teachers often **extol** the benefits of reading every single day.

**FACADE** *(noun)* | বাহ্যিক চেহারা, মিথ্যা ভাব, সামনের দিক
The front of a building; an outward appearance hiding the true nature.
✅ front, exterior, surface, pretence, mask, show, false appearance
❌ reality, interior, truth, genuine nature, authentic self, core
📝 His confident manner was a **facade** hiding deep uncertainty underneath.

**FAWN** *(verb)* | তোষামোদ করা, খুশি করতে মরিয়া হওয়া
To seek favour by flattery; to behave obsequiously toward someone.
✅ flatter, grovel, toady, be sycophantic, curry favour, ingratiate
❌ criticize, stand up to, oppose, ignore, show independence, challenge
📝 He was embarrassed watching his colleagues **fawn** over the visiting celebrity.

**FIASCO** *(noun)* | সম্পূর্ণ ব্যর্থতা, বিপর্যয়, হাস্যকর ব্যর্থতা
A complete failure, especially a ludicrous or humiliating one.
✅ disaster, failure, catastrophe, debacle, flop, collapse, ruin
❌ success, triumph, achievement, victory, accomplishment, breakthrough
📝 The launch event was an embarrassing **fiasco** that made headlines for all wrong reasons.

**FORBEAR** *(verb)* | সহ্য করা, নিজেকে সংযত রাখা
To restrain oneself from doing or saying something; to hold back.
✅ refrain, hold back, restrain, resist, abstain, stop oneself, desist
❌ indulge, proceed, continue, act, say, express, carry on
📝 She managed to **forbear** from commenting even when she strongly disagreed.

**FOREBODING** *(noun)* | অশুভ পূর্বাভাস, অমঙ্গলের আশঙ্কা
A feeling that something bad will happen; an omen of coming evil.
✅ premonition, dread, anxiety, omen, warning, apprehension, doom
❌ hope, confidence, optimism, assurance, calm, expectation of good
📝 She had a strange **foreboding** all morning before hearing the bad news.

**GERMANE** *(adjective)* | প্রাসঙ্গিক, বিষয়সম্পর্কিত, উপযুক্ত
Relevant and appropriate to the matter in hand; pertinent and fitting.
✅ relevant, pertinent, applicable, appropriate, fitting, apt, related
❌ irrelevant, unrelated, inapplicable, beside the point, off-topic
📝 Her comment was entirely **germane** to the question being discussed.

**GLEAN** *(verb)* | সামান্য সামান্য করে সংগ্রহ করা
To gather information or material from various sources in small amounts.
✅ gather, collect, pick up, acquire, harvest, extract, obtain, find
❌ waste, scatter, lose, overlook, discard, ignore, abandon
📝 She managed to **glean** enough information from the reports to draw conclusions.

**GRATIFY** *(verb)* | সন্তুষ্ট করা, আনন্দিত করা, পূরণ করা
To give someone pleasure or satisfaction; to fulfil a desire or wish.
✅ satisfy, please, fulfil, delight, appease, indulge, accommodate
❌ dissatisfy, disappoint, frustrate, annoy, deny, refuse, upset
📝 It **gratified** her enormously to see her students winning national awards.

**GRIEVANCE** *(noun)* | অভিযোগ, অভিমান, অন্যায়ের কষ্ট
A real or imagined wrong causing resentment; a formal complaint.
✅ complaint, objection, protest, resentment, injustice, wrong, issue
❌ satisfaction, contentment, gratitude, praise, approval, pleasure
📝 Workers submitted a formal **grievance** over the new overtime policy.

**HEGEMONY** *(noun)* | আধিপত্য, কর্তৃত্ব, প্রভাবশালী অবস্থান
Leadership or dominance, especially of one country or group over others.
✅ dominance, leadership, control, supremacy, authority, power, command
❌ subordination, weakness, dependence, submission, equality, balance
📝 The empire maintained its **hegemony** through a combination of trade and military power.

**HYPERBOLE** *(noun)* | অতিরঞ্জন, অত্যুক্তি
Exaggerated statements not meant to be taken literally; overstatement.
✅ exaggeration, overstatement, embellishment, overemphasis, excess
❌ understatement, litotes, plain statement, truth, accuracy
📝 Saying "I've told you a million times" is a classic example of **hyperbole**.

**IDIOSYNCRASY** *(noun)* | ব্যক্তিগত বৈশিষ্ট্য, স্বতন্ত্র অভ্যাস
A mode of behaviour or thinking peculiar to an individual; an oddity.
✅ quirk, peculiarity, oddity, eccentricity, mannerism, habit, trait
❌ conformity, normality, standard behaviour, convention, commonplace
📝 His habit of underlining every sentence was just one of his many **idiosyncrasies**.

**INCISIVE** *(adjective)* | তীক্ষ্ণ, ধারালো, সরাসরি বক্তব্য
Intelligently analytical and clear-thinking; sharp and concise in thought.
✅ sharp, keen, piercing, clear, penetrating, astute, direct, acute
❌ vague, unclear, dull, rambling, unfocused, shallow, muddled
📝 Her **incisive** analysis of the data impressed even the senior economists.

**INCONGRUITY** *(noun)* | অসামঞ্জস্য, অমিল, বেমানানভাব
The state of being incongruous; inconsistency; lack of harmony.
✅ inconsistency, mismatch, incompatibility, absurdity, contrast
❌ consistency, harmony, compatibility, congruence, fit, agreement
📝 The **incongruity** of wearing formal shoes with gym clothes caught everyone's eye.

**INDOLENCE** *(noun)* | অলসতা, কর্মবিমুখতা, নিষ্ক্রিয়তা
Habitual laziness or disinclination to exertion; sloth.
✅ laziness, sloth, sluggishness, inertia, lethargy, idleness, torpor
❌ diligence, industriousness, energy, activity, hard work, motivation
📝 His **indolence** cost him three job opportunities that year.

**INEXORABLE** *(adjective)* | অনিবার্য, থামানো যায় না, নিষ্ঠুরভাবে অবিরাম
Not to be persuaded, moved, or stopped; relentless and unyielding.
✅ relentless, unstoppable, inevitable, unceasing, implacable, merciless
❌ stoppable, flexible, avoidable, manageable, yielding, preventable
📝 The **inexorable** advance of technology continues to reshape every industry.

**INFER** *(verb)* | অনুমান করা, সিদ্ধান্তে পৌঁছানো
To deduce or conclude from evidence and reasoning; to imply.
✅ deduce, conclude, gather, assume, reason, understand, derive
❌ state directly, assert, declare, announce, say explicitly
📝 From the evidence, it was possible to **infer** that the meeting had been planned.

**INGENUOUS** *(adjective)* | সহজ, সরল, নিষ্পাপ, বিশ্বাসপ্রবণ
Innocent and unsuspecting; naively simple; lacking guile or cunning.
✅ naive, innocent, simple, trusting, candid, open, artless, sincere
❌ sophisticated, cunning, deceptive, sly, worldly, devious, guarded
📝 His **ingenuous** expression made it hard to believe he was being dishonest.

**INHERENT** *(adjective)* | সহজাত, অন্তর্নিহিত, মূলগত
Existing in something as a permanent or essential attribute.
✅ innate, intrinsic, natural, built-in, fundamental, essential, inborn
❌ acquired, external, added, cultivated, learned, imposed, incidental
📝 There is an **inherent** tension between freedom and security in any society.

**IRREFUTABLE** *(adjective)* | অখণ্ডনীয়, প্রমাণের বাইরে, নিশ্চিত
Impossible to deny or disprove; undeniable and unquestionable.
✅ undeniable, indisputable, conclusive, absolute, definitive, certain
❌ questionable, deniable, debatable, doubtful, refutable, uncertain
📝 The DNA evidence provided **irrefutable** proof of his innocence.

**JUDICIOUS** *(adjective)* | বিচক্ষণ, সুচিন্তিত সিদ্ধান্তগ্রহণকারী
Having or showing good judgement; wise and sensible in approach.
✅ wise, sensible, prudent, sound, thoughtful, careful, shrewd
❌ foolish, unwise, careless, rash, imprudent, reckless, impulsive
📝 A **judicious** use of resources helped the team complete the project under budget.

**MEANDER** *(verb)* | এঁকেবেঁকে যাওয়া, ঘুরে ঘুরে হাঁটা
To follow a winding course; to wander or move without fixed direction.
✅ wander, wind, ramble, drift, zigzag, roam, snake, stray
❌ go straight, march directly, take a direct route, proceed, go ahead
📝 The river **meandered** lazily through the valley before reaching the sea.

**MEDDLE** *(verb)* | নাক গলানো, অন্যের বিষয়ে হস্তক্ষেপ করা
To interfere in or busy oneself with things that are not one's concern.
✅ interfere, intrude, pry, tamper, intervene, stick one's nose in
❌ stay out, mind one's own business, refrain, respect boundaries
📝 He was always **meddling** in affairs that were none of his business.

**OBDURATE** *(adjective)* | অনমনীয়, একরোখা, অনুশোচনাহীন
Stubbornly refusing to change one's opinion or action; unmoved.
✅ stubborn, inflexible, unyielding, hardened, unrepentant, adamant
❌ flexible, yielding, remorseful, open-minded, compliant, persuadable
📝 Despite every argument presented, he remained completely **obdurate**.

**OSTENTATIOUS** *(adjective)* | দেখানোপনা, জাঁকজমকপূর্ণ, ঐশ্বর্যের প্রদর্শনী
Characterized by a vulgar display of wealth and success; showy.
✅ showy, pretentious, flashy, extravagant, flamboyant, loud, gaudy
❌ modest, understated, simple, humble, restrained, plain, quiet
📝 The **ostentatious** decoration of the hotel lobby was widely mocked by critics.

**PARADOX** *(noun)* | স্ববিরোধী বক্তব্য, আপাত বিরোধ
A seemingly contradictory statement that may actually be true; an enigma.
✅ contradiction, enigma, irony, puzzle, conundrum, mystery, anomaly
❌ straightforward fact, clear statement, simple truth, certainty
📝 It is a **paradox** that the more choices we have, the less happy we sometimes feel.

**PARSIMONIOUS** *(adjective)* | কৃপণ, অত্যন্ত মিতব্যয়ী, অনুদার
Unwilling to spend money or use resources; extremely frugal; miserly.
✅ stingy, miserly, mean, tight-fisted, niggardly, frugal, ungenerous
❌ generous, extravagant, lavish, open-handed, charitable, liberal
📝 His **parsimonious** nature meant he never bought himself anything new.

**PENSIVE** *(adjective)* | ভাবুক, চিন্তামগ্ন, গভীর ভাবনায় মগ্ন
Engaged in, involving, or reflecting deep or serious thought; thoughtful.
✅ thoughtful, reflective, contemplative, meditative, introspective, wistful
❌ carefree, unthinking, shallow, thoughtless, impulsive, cheerful
📝 She sat by the window with a **pensive** expression, deep in thought.

**PERENNIAL** *(adjective)* | বছরের পর বছর চলা, স্থায়ী, চিরস্থায়ী
Lasting or existing for a long or apparently infinite time; enduring.
✅ permanent, lasting, enduring, recurring, constant, eternal, chronic
❌ temporary, seasonal, short-lived, fleeting, occasional, transient
📝 The shortage of housing in the city is a **perennial** problem with no easy solution.

**PERFUNCTORY** *(adjective)* | উপর উপর করা, মনোযোগহীন, দায়সারা
Carried out with a minimum of effort; lacking care or interest; routine.
✅ cursory, superficial, careless, half-hearted, minimal, hasty, shallow
❌ thorough, careful, detailed, attentive, diligent, conscientious
📝 His **perfunctory** review of the document missed several critical errors.

**PREVAIL** *(verb)* | জয়ী হওয়া, বিজয়ী হওয়া, প্রচলিত থাকা
To be victorious; to prove more powerful; to be widespread or current.
✅ win, succeed, triumph, dominate, overcome, be common, persist
❌ fail, lose, succumb, be overcome, be defeated, surrender, yield
📝 Good sense must **prevail** if any agreement is to be reached by both parties.

**PROBITY** *(noun)* | সততা, ন্যায়পরায়ণতা, নৈতিক দৃঢ়তা
The quality of having strong moral principles; complete honesty.
✅ integrity, honesty, virtue, honour, uprightness, righteousness, ethics
❌ dishonesty, corruption, fraud, immorality, duplicity, deception
📝 His **probity** in handling public funds set a standard few could match.

**PROCRASTINATE** *(verb)* | কাজ পিছিয়ে দেওয়া, টালবাহানা করা
To delay or postpone action; to put off doing something repeatedly.
✅ delay, postpone, put off, defer, stall, dawdle, dither, hesitate
❌ act promptly, proceed, get on with, do at once, be decisive
📝 She had a habit of **procrastinating** on tasks until they became urgent crises.

**RANCOUR** *(noun)* | তীব্র বিদ্বেষ, দীর্ঘস্থায়ী ঘৃণা
Bitterness or resentfulness; a long-lasting feeling of ill will.
✅ bitterness, resentment, hatred, hostility, spite, animosity, malice
❌ forgiveness, goodwill, warmth, kindness, harmony, affection
📝 The **rancour** between the two colleagues made every meeting uncomfortable.

**RIGOROUS** *(adjective)* | কঠোর, যথাযথ, অত্যন্ত সতর্ক
Extremely thorough and careful; not allowing weaknesses; demanding.
✅ thorough, strict, demanding, precise, exact, exhaustive, severe
❌ lenient, lax, careless, easy, loose, sloppy, flexible, casual
📝 The **rigorous** training programme produced the most disciplined team in years.

**SAGACITY** *(noun)* | বিচক্ষণতা, প্রজ্ঞা, সুচিন্তিত বিচার
The quality of being sagacious; keenness of intelligence; wisdom.
✅ wisdom, insight, intelligence, astuteness, shrewdness, perception
❌ stupidity, ignorance, naivety, foolishness, imprudence, dullness
📝 Her **sagacity** in managing the crisis earned her lasting respect from all sides.

**SANCTION** *(noun/verb)* | অনুমোদন; নিষেধাজ্ঞা; শাস্তি
Official permission for an action; a penalty for disobeying a rule.
✅ approval, permission, authorisation, penalty, punishment, prohibition
❌ prohibition, rejection, denial, reward, encouragement, support
📝 The board gave its **sanction** to the proposal after months of deliberation.

**SARDONIC** *(adjective)* | তীক্ষ্ণ ব্যঙ্গ, কটু রসিকতা, শ্লেষাত্মক
Grimly mocking or cynical; having a sardonic wit; bitterly sarcastic.
✅ sarcastic, cynical, mocking, scornful, wry, derisive, disdainful
❌ kind, sincere, complimentary, genuine, warm, admiring, flattering
📝 He gave a **sardonic** smile when told the plan had finally been approved.

**SEVER** *(verb)* | কেটে আলাদা করা, বিচ্ছিন্ন করা, সম্পর্ক ছিন্ন করা
To put an end to a connection; to cut through something completely.
✅ cut, separate, divide, disconnect, break, sever, split, terminate
❌ connect, join, maintain, preserve, continue, uphold, keep
📝 The two countries chose to **sever** diplomatic ties following the incident.

**SOMNOLENT** *(adjective)* | ঘুম ঘুম ভাব, অর্ধ-ঘুমন্ত
Sleepy and drowsy; tending to cause sleepiness; sluggish.
✅ sleepy, drowsy, sluggish, lethargic, torpid, languid, heavy
❌ alert, wide-awake, energetic, lively, attentive, vigorous, sharp
📝 The long lecture and warm room made the entire audience feel **somnolent**.

**SORDID** *(adjective)* | নোংরা, ঘৃণ্য, নৈতিকভাবে নিচু
Involving ignoble actions and motives; dirty and filthy; morally base.
✅ dirty, filthy, nasty, disreputable, shameful, corrupt, despicable
❌ clean, honourable, noble, decent, admirable, virtuous, pure
📝 The **sordid** details of the scandal made front-page news for weeks.

**SPECIOUS** *(adjective)* | বাহ্যত সত্য কিন্তু আসলে মিথ্যা
Superficially plausible, but actually wrong; deceptively attractive.
✅ misleading, deceptive, false, sophistic, plausible-sounding but wrong
❌ genuine, truthful, authentic, valid, honest, accurate, reliable
📝 The lawyer's **specious** argument initially convinced many in the courtroom.

**SUBVERT** *(verb)* | উল্টে দেওয়া, বিপর্যস্ত করা, ধ্বংস করা
To undermine the power and authority of an established system.
✅ undermine, destabilize, overthrow, sabotage, corrupt, weaken, ruin
❌ support, strengthen, uphold, reinforce, build, maintain, defend
📝 The group attempted to **subvert** the election results through misinformation.

**TANGENTIAL** *(adjective)* | অপ্রাসঙ্গিক, পরোক্ষভাবে সম্পর্কিত
Diverging from a previous course; related to but not at the centre.
✅ peripheral, incidental, indirect, beside the point, tangential, unrelated
❌ central, relevant, core, essential, primary, direct, key, fundamental
📝 His comments were largely **tangential** to the main issue being discussed.

**TRANSGRESSION** *(noun)* | লঙ্ঘন, অপরাধ, নীতি ভঙ্গ
The violation of a law or rule; an act that goes beyond accepted limits.
✅ violation, breach, offence, sin, crime, infringement, wrongdoing
❌ compliance, obedience, lawfulness, virtue, following rules, adherence
📝 Even a minor **transgression** of the safety rules had serious consequences.

**TRANSIENT** *(adjective)* | ক্ষণস্থায়ী, অস্থায়ী, সাময়িক
Lasting only for a short time; not permanent; passing quickly.
✅ temporary, fleeting, brief, short-lived, passing, ephemeral, momentary
❌ permanent, lasting, enduring, eternal, perpetual, stable, fixed
📝 Fame can be deeply **transient** — celebrated one year and forgotten the next.

**TURBULENCE** *(noun)* | অশান্তি, উথালপাতাল, বিক্ষোভ
A state of conflict, confusion, or disorder; irregular motion of fluids.
✅ chaos, upheaval, disorder, unrest, turmoil, disruption, instability
❌ calm, stability, peace, order, tranquility, stillness, harmony
📝 Political **turbulence** made it almost impossible to plan for the future.

**UNEQUIVOCAL** *(adjective)* | স্পষ্ট, নিঃসন্দেহ, দ্ব্যর্থহীন
Leaving no doubt; clear and unambiguous; expressed without doubt.
✅ clear, definite, absolute, unambiguous, certain, explicit, direct
❌ equivocal, vague, uncertain, ambiguous, unclear, doubtful, wavering
📝 The statement was an **unequivocal** rejection of any further negotiations.

**VENERATE** *(verb)* | শ্রদ্ধা করা, পূজা করা, সম্মান করা
To regard with great respect and reverence; to honour with worship.
✅ revere, worship, respect, admire, honour, esteem, adore, cherish
❌ despise, disrespect, mock, dishonour, ridicule, scorn, belittle
📝 The founder is still deeply **venerated** by everyone in the organisation.

**VIABLE** *(adjective)* | কার্যকর, সম্ভাবনাময়, টিকে থাকতে পারে এমন
Capable of working successfully; feasible; able to survive or function.
✅ workable, feasible, practical, possible, sustainable, functional
❌ unworkable, impractical, impossible, unsustainable, unfeasible
📝 The committee decided that only two of the five proposals were truly **viable**.

**VINDICTIVE** *(adjective)* | প্রতিশোধমূলক, ক্ষমাহীন, বদলা নিতে ইচ্ছুক
Having or showing a strong or unreasoning desire for revenge.
✅ vengeful, spiteful, unforgiving, resentful, malicious, retaliatory
❌ forgiving, magnanimous, merciful, tolerant, understanding, kind
📝 Her **vindictive** response to the criticism surprised even those who knew her.

**VOCIFEROUS** *(adjective)* | উচ্চকণ্ঠ, জোরে দাবি করা, সোচ্চার
Expressing or characterized by vehement opinions; clamorous; noisy.
✅ loud, noisy, clamorous, outspoken, insistent, vocal, strong-voiced
❌ quiet, silent, reserved, restrained, subtle, understated, calm

**ENHANCE** *(verb)* | বৃদ্ধি করা, উন্নত করা
To intensify, increase, or further improve the quality, value, or extent.
✅ improve, increase, boost, strengthen, augment, elevate, upgrade
❌ diminish, reduce, weaken, worsen, decrease, undermine, lessen
📝 Regular daily practice significantly **enhances** both skill and overall performance.

**ENORMOUS** *(adjective)* | বিশাল, অতিকায়
Very large in size, quantity, or extent; immensely great or huge.
✅ huge, vast, immense, gigantic, massive, colossal, tremendous, great
❌ tiny, small, minute, little, insignificant, petite, diminutive, minor
📝 The city council approved plans to build an **enormous** new sports stadium.

**ENTREATY** *(noun)* | অনুনয়, আবেদন
An earnest or humble request; an act of asking urgently and sincerely.
✅ plea, appeal, request, petition, supplication, imploration, begging
❌ demand, order, command, decree, insistence, compulsion, threat
📝 She made a tearful and heartfelt **entreaty** to the committee for more time.

**EQUANIMITY** *(noun)* | মানসিক শান্তি, প্রশান্তি
Mental calmness and composure, especially in difficult situations; poise.
✅ composure, calmness, serenity, tranquility, poise, balance, steadiness
❌ anxiety, agitation, panic, distress, turmoil, nervousness, instability
📝 He faced every crisis throughout his career with remarkable **equanimity** and grace.

**ESTIMABLE** *(adjective)* | প্রশংসনীয়, সম্মানযোগ্য
Worthy of great respect and admiration; deserving estimation and regard.
✅ admirable, respectable, praiseworthy, commendable, worthy, honourable
❌ disreputable, contemptible, shameful, unworthy, despicable, ignoble
📝 She has many **estimable** qualities that make her an excellent candidate for leadership.

**ETERNAL** *(adjective)* | চিরন্তন, অনন্ত
Lasting or existing forever; without beginning or end; perpetual.
✅ everlasting, permanent, perpetual, endless, immortal, timeless, infinite
❌ temporary, fleeting, mortal, finite, brief, transient, short-lived
📝 The two old friends promised each other a bond of **eternal** and unbreakable friendship.

**PERVASIVE** *(adjective)* | সর্বব্যাপী, ব্যাপক
Spreading widely throughout an area or group of people; prevalent everywhere.
✅ widespread, omnipresent, extensive, prevalent, rampant, ubiquitous
❌ limited, rare, isolated, absent, scarce, contained, localized
📝 Corruption had become deeply **pervasive** at every single level of the administration.

**EVIDENT** *(adjective)* | স্পষ্ট, প্রকাশিত
Plain or obvious; clearly seen or understood without any doubt or need for proof.
✅ obvious, clear, apparent, plain, visible, manifest, undeniable, patent
❌ unclear, hidden, obscure, subtle, doubtful, ambiguous, concealed
📝 It was entirely **evident** from his pale face and shaking hands that he was unwell.

**EXAGGERATE** *(verb)* | অতিরঞ্জিত করা, বাড়িয়ে বলা
To represent something as being larger, better, or worse than it really is.
✅ overstate, magnify, amplify, embellish, inflate, overplay, blow up
❌ understate, minimise, downplay, reduce, moderate, tone down
📝 He always tends to **exaggerate** the difficulties of his job to gain sympathy.

**EXCEPTIONAL** *(adjective)* | ব্যতিক্রমী, অসাধারণ
Unusually good or outstanding; forming an exception; extraordinary.
✅ outstanding, extraordinary, remarkable, superior, excellent, unparalleled
❌ ordinary, average, mediocre, typical, common, unremarkable, standard
📝 The young athlete showed truly **exceptional** talent from a very early age indeed.

**EXECRATION** *(noun)* | ঘৃণা, অভিশাপ, তীব্র নিন্দা
The act of feeling or expressing great hatred; a curse or denunciation.
✅ hatred, loathing, curse, condemnation, denunciation, abhorrence, abomination
❌ praise, blessing, admiration, approval, commendation, celebration
📝 His cowardly actions drew widespread **execration** and contempt from all sides.

**EXHILARATED** *(adjective)* | উত্তেজিত, আনন্দিত, উজ্জীবিত
Feeling very happy, animated, and elated; filled with a lively and exciting joy.
✅ elated, thrilled, excited, overjoyed, animated, ecstatic, energized
❌ depressed, dejected, bored, sad, dull, miserable, listless, downcast
📝 She felt completely **exhilarated** after crossing the finish line and winning the race.

**EXOTIC** *(adjective)* | বিদেশি, অপরিচিত, আকর্ষণীয়
Originating in or characteristic of a distant foreign country; strikingly unusual.
✅ foreign, unusual, strange, outlandish, unfamiliar, striking, tropical
❌ native, ordinary, familiar, local, common, domestic, everyday
📝 They spent their honeymoon exploring a beautiful and truly **exotic** island destination.

**EXPIRE** *(verb)* | মেয়াদ শেষ হওয়া, মারা যাওয়া
To come to an end; to cease to be valid; to die.
✅ end, lapse, run out, terminate, conclude, die, elapse, become void
❌ begin, renew, continue, start, extend, persist, activate, last
📝 His travel passport will **expire** at the end of next month and must be renewed.

**EXPOSE** *(verb)* | উন্মোচন করা, প্রকাশ করা
To make something visible by uncovering it; to reveal something hidden.
✅ reveal, uncover, disclose, show, unmask, bring to light, lay bare
❌ hide, conceal, cover, protect, shield, obscure, keep secret
📝 The courageous journalist **exposed** the massive corruption scandal to the public.

**EXTRANEOUS** *(adjective)* | অপ্রাসঙ্গিক, বাইরের
Irrelevant or unrelated to the subject being dealt with; coming from outside.
✅ irrelevant, unrelated, unnecessary, peripheral, superfluous, beside the point
❌ relevant, related, essential, pertinent, necessary, central, core
📝 Please remove all **extraneous** details from the report before submitting it.

**EXTRAVAGANT** *(adjective)* | বিলাসী, অপচয়ী
Lacking restraint in spending money or using resources; excessive.
✅ wasteful, lavish, excessive, profligate, indulgent, spendthrift, reckless
❌ frugal, economical, thrifty, modest, restrained, careful, prudent
📝 He was criticised for leading a shamelessly **extravagant** and wasteful lifestyle.

**EXUDE** *(verb)* | প্রকাশ পাওয়া, ঝরানো
To display an emotion or quality strongly; to ooze out slowly.
✅ radiate, emit, project, display, show, emanate, ooze, give off
❌ hide, suppress, conceal, hold back, mask, restrain, cover up
📝 She walks into every room and instantly **exudes** confidence and natural authority.

## 🔤 F (New)

**FABULIST** *(noun)* | মিথ্যাবাদী, গল্পবাজ
A person who invents elaborate falsehoods; a liar; a teller of fables.
✅ liar, storyteller, fabricator, inventor, deceiver, spinner of tales
❌ truth-teller, honest person, factual reporter, journalist, realist
📝 He was widely known among friends as a harmless but incorrigible **fabulist**.

**FABULOUS** *(adjective)* | চমৎকার, অসাধারণ
Extraordinarily good or attractive; very great; known only from legend.
✅ wonderful, amazing, extraordinary, marvellous, spectacular, superb
❌ ordinary, terrible, awful, dreadful, unremarkable, mediocre, poor
📝 We had the most absolutely **fabulous** holiday we have ever experienced together.

**FAIN** *(adjective/adverb)* | ইচ্ছুক, আগ্রহী (সেকালের ব্যবহার)
Willing or eager under the circumstances; pleased to do something (archaic).
✅ willing, eager, ready, pleased, content, inclined, disposed
❌ unwilling, reluctant, disinclined, averse, loath, opposed
📝 The weary traveller was **fain** to accept any shelter offered on that cold night.

**FAKE** *(adjective/noun)* | নকল, জাল
Not genuine; counterfeit; a person or thing that is not what it seems.
✅ counterfeit, false, fraudulent, bogus, artificial, phony, sham
❌ genuine, authentic, real, original, legitimate, true, verified
📝 The detective immediately noticed she was wearing an obviously **fake** diamond ring.

**FANCY** *(adjective/verb)* | জমকালো; পছন্দ করা
Elaborate or highly decorated; to feel attracted to or desire something.
✅ elaborate, ornate, decorative, stylish, extravagant, like, desire
❌ plain, simple, ordinary, basic, unattractive, dislike, reject
📝 The hotel suite was lavishly decorated in a very **fancy** and ornate style.

**FATAL** *(adjective)* | মারাত্মক, প্রাণঘাতী
Causing death; leading to failure or disaster; having extremely grave results.
✅ deadly, lethal, mortal, catastrophic, disastrous, terminal, destructive
❌ harmless, safe, beneficial, lifesaving, healing, minor, benign
📝 The tragic road accident ultimately proved entirely **fatal** for the young driver.

**FEASIBLE** *(adjective)* | সম্ভাব্য, কার্যকর
Possible to do easily or conveniently; likely and probable; practicable.
✅ possible, practicable, viable, workable, achievable, realistic, doable
❌ impossible, impractical, unworkable, unrealistic, unachievable, unfeasible
📝 After careful study, the proposed plan seemed entirely **feasible** and well-conceived.

**FEISTY** *(adjective)* | তেজি, সাহসী, জেদি
Having or showing a lively aggressiveness; spirited and plucky in character.
✅ spirited, plucky, bold, determined, tenacious, fiery, lively, spunky
❌ timid, meek, passive, submissive, weak, cowardly, gentle, shy
📝 Despite her small size, she has a wonderfully **feisty** and determined personality.

**FERTILE** *(adjective)* | উর্বর, ফলপ্রসূ
Producing or capable of producing abundant vegetation or crops; productive.
✅ productive, rich, fruitful, lush, abundant, creative, generative
❌ barren, sterile, unproductive, arid, infertile, dry, depleted
📝 The river delta region has some of the most **fertile** agricultural land in the country.

**FICTION** *(noun)* | কল্পকাহিনী, কাল্পনিক রচনা
Literature in the form of prose that describes imaginary events and people.
✅ novel, story, narrative, tale, fantasy, invention, fabrication
❌ fact, reality, truth, non-fiction, history, documentary, account
📝 She writes compelling **fiction** novels that explore the complexities of human emotion.

**FLAW** *(noun)* | ত্রুটি, দোষ
A mark, fault, or defect that spoils the appearance or quality of something.
✅ defect, fault, imperfection, weakness, error, blemish, crack, shortcoming
❌ perfection, strength, asset, virtue, advantage, quality, excellence
📝 The defence lawyer identified a critical **flaw** in the prosecutor's entire argument.

**FRACTION** *(noun)* | অংশ, ভগ্নাংশ
A numerical quantity that is not a whole number; a small part or amount.
✅ portion, part, segment, piece, bit, slice, section, snippet, share
❌ whole, total, entirety, all, complete amount, sum, everything
📝 Only a tiny **fraction** of the planned construction work had actually been completed.

**FRENZY** *(noun)* | উন্মত্ততা, উত্তেজনা
A state or period of uncontrolled excitement or wild behaviour; mania.
✅ mania, excitement, hysteria, frenzy, agitation, furor, uproar, craze
❌ calm, peace, composure, tranquility, serenity, stillness, order
📝 The ecstatic fans were sent into an absolute **frenzy** after the last-minute winning goal.

**FRIENDLY** *(adjective)* | বন্ধুত্বপূর্ণ, মিলনশীল
Kind and pleasant; showing or expressing kindness and goodwill; amiable.
✅ amiable, warm, kind, affable, sociable, welcoming, pleasant, cordial
❌ hostile, unfriendly, cold, rude, distant, aloof, antagonistic
📝 Every single member of staff was exceptionally **friendly** and incredibly helpful.

**FRIGID** *(adjective)* | হিমশীতল, অত্যন্ত ঠান্ডা
Very cold; lacking warmth of feeling; unfriendly or emotionally cold.
✅ freezing, icy, arctic, bitter, cold, glacial, chilly, frosty
❌ warm, hot, tropical, balmy, burning, scorching, comfortable
📝 The **frigid** north wind cut through their clothing and chilled everyone to the bone.

**GALVANIZE** *(verb)* | উদ্দীপিত করা, জাগিয়ে তোলা
To shock or excite someone into taking action; to stimulate into activity.
✅ energize, stimulate, motivate, inspire, rouse, activate, spur, jolt
❌ demotivate, discourage, pacify, calm, suppress, deter, deaden
📝 The passionate speech successfully **galvanized** the entire crowd into immediate action.

**GARGANTUAN** *(adjective)* | বিশালাকার, অতিকায়
Enormous; gigantic; of huge and extraordinary size or degree.
✅ enormous, massive, huge, colossal, gigantic, immense, vast, titanic
❌ tiny, small, miniature, minute, petite, insignificant, diminutive
📝 They were given a truly **gargantuan** and seemingly impossible task to accomplish.

**GATHER** *(verb)* | জড়ো করা, একত্রিত হওয়া
To come together; to collect or accumulate things; to assemble in one place.
✅ collect, assemble, accumulate, amass, congregate, convene, group
❌ scatter, disperse, separate, spread, divide, distribute, disband
📝 Large crowds of curious people **gathered** in the town square to watch the ceremony.

**GAUDY** *(adjective)* | জমকালো, চোখ-ধাঁধানো কিন্তু রুচিহীন
Extravagantly bright or showy in a way that is considered tasteless.
✅ flashy, garish, showy, loud, tacky, ostentatious, vulgar, over-the-top
❌ tasteful, elegant, subtle, refined, understated, classy, dignified
📝 She wore a loudly **gaudy** outfit to the formal dinner that drew stares from everyone.

**GAUNT** *(adjective)* | শীর্ণ, রোগা, হাড়জিরজিরে
Very thin and bony in appearance, typically due to illness or starvation.
✅ thin, lean, skinny, bony, emaciated, haggard, angular, wasted
❌ plump, healthy, well-fed, robust, full, fleshy, chubby, rounded
📝 After months of serious illness, he emerged looking pale, weak, and deeply **gaunt**.

**GENERATE** *(verb)* | উৎপন্ন করা, তৈরি করা
To produce or create something; to cause something to arise or come about.
✅ produce, create, make, cause, originate, bring about, yield, give rise to
❌ destroy, eliminate, reduce, stop, prevent, suppress, cease, end
📝 The new solar farm will **generate** enough clean electricity for ten thousand homes.

**GENEROUS** *(adjective)* | উদার, দানশীল
Showing a readiness to give more than is strictly necessary; magnanimous.
✅ liberal, giving, charitable, magnanimous, unselfish, bountiful, kind
❌ stingy, mean, selfish, miserly, tight-fisted, greedy, parsimonious
📝 He is remarkably **generous** with both his time and his considerable personal wealth.

**GENESIS** *(noun)* | উৎপত্তি, সূচনা
The origin or mode of formation of something; a beginning; a creation.
✅ origin, beginning, birth, start, creation, source, inception, root
❌ end, conclusion, death, termination, destruction, finish, demise
📝 This historic agreement marks the true **genesis** of an entirely new political era.

**GENUINE** *(adjective)* | প্রকৃত, খাঁটি, আন্তরিক
Truly what something is said to be; authentic; sincere in feelings.
✅ authentic, real, true, sincere, honest, legitimate, bona fide, actual
❌ fake, artificial, insincere, counterfeit, false, phony, pretended
📝 She showed **genuine** concern and deep empathy for the struggles of others.

**GIGANTIC** *(adjective)* | বিশাল, দৈত্যাকার
Of very great size; enormous; much larger than the usual or expected size.
✅ enormous, huge, massive, colossal, vast, immense, gargantuan, giant
❌ tiny, small, miniature, little, petite, minute, diminutive, compact
📝 The construction team built a truly **gigantic** tower that dominated the entire skyline.

**GLOOMY** *(adjective)* | বিষণ্ণ, অন্ধকারাচ্ছন্ন
Dark or poorly lit, especially so as to appear depressing; pessimistic.
✅ dark, dismal, depressing, dim, dreary, bleak, cheerless, melancholy
❌ bright, cheerful, sunny, optimistic, lively, happy, vibrant, light
📝 The heavy grey sky looked deeply **gloomy** and threatening in the hours before the storm.

**GLORIOUS** *(adjective)* | মহিমান্বিত, অসাধারণ সুন্দর
Having, worthy of, or bringing fame and admiration; wonderfully beautiful.
✅ magnificent, splendid, wonderful, superb, brilliant, illustrious, radiant
❌ shameful, ignoble, dull, ordinary, disgraced, inglorious, mediocre
📝 The national team celebrated their long-awaited and truly **glorious** championship victory.

**GORGEOUS** *(adjective)* | অপরূপ সুন্দর, দারুণ
Beautiful in a striking or impressive way; very pleasant, attractive, or enjoyable.
✅ beautiful, stunning, magnificent, lovely, dazzling, ravishing, breathtaking
❌ ugly, plain, unattractive, hideous, unsightly, dull, ordinary
📝 She looked absolutely **gorgeous** in the elegant deep blue gown she had chosen.

**GRAVE** *(adjective)* | গুরুতর, ভয়ঙ্কর
Giving cause for alarm; serious and worrying; solemn and weighty.
✅ serious, critical, severe, solemn, weighty, alarming, dire, urgent
❌ trivial, minor, light, cheerful, unimportant, insignificant, mild
📝 The doctor warned that the patient's condition had become dangerously **grave**.

**GRIM** *(adjective)* | কঠোর, অন্ধকারাচ্ছন্ন, ভয়াবহ
Very serious or gloomy; forbidding; causing feelings of worry or despair.
✅ severe, stern, harsh, bleak, forbidding, fierce, sinister, menacing
❌ cheerful, bright, pleasant, mild, kind, gentle, encouraging, hopeful
📝 The news coming from the war zone remained consistently **grim** and deeply worrying.

**GRISLY** *(adjective)* | ভয়ঙ্কর, বীভৎস
Causing horror or disgust; ghastly and frightening; involving blood and death.
✅ gruesome, ghastly, horrible, horrifying, macabre, dreadful, shocking
❌ pleasant, beautiful, lovely, clean, comforting, delightful, appealing
📝 The investigators were deeply disturbed by the truly **grisly** nature of the crime scene.

## 🔤 H (New)

**HAGGARD** *(adjective)* | ক্লান্ত, শীর্ণ, বিধ্বস্ত
Looking exhausted and unwell, especially from sustained worry or effort.
✅ gaunt, worn, exhausted, drawn, hollow-eyed, tired, pale, wasted
❌ fresh, healthy, vibrant, energetic, glowing, radiant, well-rested
📝 He looked utterly **haggard** and pale after the incredibly long and demanding journey.

**HANDY** *(adjective)* | উপযোগী, সুবিধাজনক
Convenient to handle or use; useful; skilled with the hands.
✅ useful, convenient, practical, helpful, accessible, skillful, dexterous
❌ inconvenient, useless, impractical, unhelpful, cumbersome, clumsy

**HAZARDOUS** *(adjective)* | বিপজ্জনক, ক্ষতিকর
Risky; dangerous; involving a risk of harm, death, or injury.
✅ dangerous, perilous, risky, unsafe, harmful, threatening, treacherous
❌ safe, harmless, secure, risk-free, protected, benign, innocuous
📝 The chemical substance is known to be highly **hazardous** to all human health.

**HEROIC** *(adjective)* | বীরত্বপূর্ণ, সাহসী
Having the qualities of a hero; brave, noble, and courageous in the extreme.
✅ brave, courageous, valiant, noble, gallant, fearless, bold, selfless
❌ cowardly, timid, ignoble, weak, selfish, spineless, fearful, unheroic
📝 The young firefighter made a completely **heroic** sacrifice to save a trapped family.

**HOSPITABLE** *(adjective)* | অতিথিপরায়ণ, উদার
Friendly and welcoming to strangers or guests; generous and warm.
✅ welcoming, friendly, generous, warm, open-hearted, accommodating
❌ inhospitable, cold, unwelcoming, hostile, unfriendly, selfish, rude
📝 The local villagers were remarkably **hospitable** to every traveller who passed through.

**HUGE** *(adjective)* | বিশাল, অতিকায়
Extremely large; enormous in size, magnitude, or quantity.
✅ enormous, massive, gigantic, immense, colossal, vast, gargantuan
❌ tiny, small, little, minute, miniature, petite, insignificant
📝 The wealthy family was known to live in a truly **huge** and imposing old mansion.

**HUMANE** *(adjective)* | মানবিক, দয়ালু
Having or showing compassion or benevolence; marked by kindness.
✅ compassionate, kind, benevolent, merciful, gentle, sympathetic, caring
❌ cruel, inhumane, brutal, ruthless, heartless, savage, callous
📝 The doctor was known for treating every patient in a deeply **humane** and dignified way.

**HUMBLE** *(adjective)* | বিনয়ী, নম্র
Having or showing a modest estimate of one's importance; low in status.
✅ modest, meek, unassuming, unpretentious, self-effacing, lowly, gentle
❌ arrogant, proud, conceited, boastful, self-important, haughty, vain
📝 Despite his enormous global fame and success, he remained genuinely **humble**.

**HUMILIATE** *(verb)* | অপমান করা, লজ্জা দেওয়া
To make someone feel ashamed and foolish by injuring their dignity and pride.
✅ embarrass, shame, disgrace, demean, degrade, mortify, belittle
❌ honour, praise, respect, dignify, uplift, celebrate, elevate, commend
📝 He coldly and deliberately **humiliated** her in front of her entire team of colleagues.

**HUMILITY** *(noun)* | বিনয়, নম্রতা
The quality of having a modest opinion of one's own importance; modesty.
✅ modesty, meekness, humbleness, unpretentiousness, self-effacement
❌ arrogance, pride, conceit, vanity, pomposity, self-importance
📝 He accepted the prestigious national award with quiet dignity and deep **humility**.

## 🔤 I (New)

**IDENTICAL** *(adjective)* | অভিন্ন, একই রকম
Similar in every detail; exactly the same; corresponding precisely.
✅ same, alike, equal, matching, indistinguishable, uniform, twin
❌ different, distinct, unlike, varied, diverse, dissimilar, unique

**IGNOBLE** *(adjective)* | লজ্জাজনক, হীন

✅ dishonourable, shameful, base, mean, contemptible, degrading, undignified
❌ noble, honourable, dignified, admirable, proud, worthy, distinguished
📝 His act of blaming others for his own mistakes was widely considered utterly **ignoble**.

**IGNORANT** *(adjective)* | অজ্ঞ, অজানা
Lacking knowledge, education, or awareness about something in particular.
✅ uneducated, uninformed, unaware, oblivious, unlearned, naive, illiterate
❌ knowledgeable, educated, aware, informed, learned, enlightened
📝 He was completely **ignorant** of the local customs and caused great offence unknowingly.

**ILLUSION** *(noun)* | বিভ্রম, মায়া, ভুল ধারণা
A false idea or belief; a deceptive appearance or impression of reality.
✅ misconception, delusion, fantasy, mirage, false belief, deception
❌ reality, truth, fact, certainty, knowledge, clarity, actuality
📝 She had been living comfortably in a pleasant **illusion** of success for years.

**IMMATURE** *(adjective)* | অপরিণত, অপ্রাপ্তবয়স্ক আচরণের
Not fully developed; exhibiting a lack of maturity; childish or premature.
✅ childish, undeveloped, juvenile, premature, puerile, unripe, naive
❌ mature, developed, adult, grown-up, responsible, sophisticated, wise
📝 His constant tantrums and sulking made him appear genuinely **immature** to everyone.

**IMPATIENT** *(adjective)* | অধৈর্য, অসহিষ্ণু
Having or showing a tendency to be quickly irritated or provoked; restless.
✅ restless, agitated, irritable, hasty, anxious, eager, intolerant
❌ patient, calm, tolerant, composed, understanding, long-suffering
📝 She grew increasingly **impatient** while waiting for the delayed bus in the cold.

**IMPERTINENT** *(adjective)* | ধৃষ্ট, অবান্তর
Not showing proper respect; rude and presumptuous; irrelevant and out of place.
✅ rude, disrespectful, insolent, impudent, cheeky, forward, presumptuous
❌ respectful, polite, relevant, appropriate, courteous, deferential
📝 The student's shockingly **impertinent** remark completely stunned the experienced teacher.

**IMPLY** *(verb)* | ইঙ্গিত করা, বোঝানো
To indicate or suggest something without actually stating it directly.
✅ suggest, indicate, hint, insinuate, intimate, signify, mean, allude to
❌ state directly, declare, assert, express clearly, specify, announce
📝 His long and deliberate silence seemed to clearly **imply** his full agreement.

**IMPORTANT** *(adjective)* | গুরুত্বপূর্ণ, প্রয়োজনীয়
Of great significance or value; likely to have a profound effect on things.
✅ significant, vital, crucial, essential, key, critical, major, weighty
❌ unimportant, insignificant, trivial, minor, irrelevant, negligible
📝 **Education** is undeniably one of the most **important** investments any society can make.

**INCLEMENT** *(adjective)* | রুক্ষ, ঝড়ো, কঠোর (আবহাওয়া)
(Of weather) unpleasantly cold or wet; (of a person) not merciful or kind.
✅ harsh, severe, stormy, rough, bitter, cold, wet, unpleasant, brutal
❌ mild, calm, pleasant, warm, gentle, fine, sunny, favourable
📝 **Inclement** weather conditions caused a long delay in the departure of all flights.

**INCONSEQUENTIAL** *(adjective)* | গুরুত্বহীন, তুচ্ছ
Not important or significant; having no noteworthy impact or consequence.
✅ trivial, unimportant, minor, negligible, insignificant, irrelevant, petty
❌ significant, important, consequential, major, meaningful, weighty
📝 His opinion on the matter was considered largely **inconsequential** by the committee.

**INCREDIBLE** *(adjective)* | অবিশ্বাস্য, অসাধারণ
Impossible to believe; extraordinary and amazing; extraordinary in degree.
✅ unbelievable, extraordinary, astonishing, amazing, implausible, fantastic
❌ believable, ordinary, credible, expected, plausible, commonplace
📝 The entire story he told sounded so completely **incredible** that no one believed a word.

**INORDINATE** *(adjective)* | অত্যধিক, মাত্রাতিরিক্ত
Unusually or disproportionately large; excessive; beyond normal limits.
✅ excessive, disproportionate, extreme, unreasonable, undue, extravagant
❌ moderate, reasonable, appropriate, proportionate, restrained, limited
📝 He spent an truly **inordinate** and unjustifiable amount of time on a minor detail.

**INTANGIBLE** *(adjective)* | অমূর্ত, অধরা
Unable to be touched or physically grasped; difficult to define or measure.
✅ abstract, incorporeal, vague, imperceptible, indefinable, immeasurable
❌ tangible, physical, concrete, real, measurable, solid, visible
📝 Trust is a fundamentally **intangible** asset that takes years and years to build.

**INTERDICT** *(verb/noun)* | নিষেধ করা; নিষেধাজ্ঞা
To prohibit or forbid something; to intercept and cut off a supply line.
✅ prohibit, forbid, ban, block, prevent, stop, restrict, intercept
❌ allow, permit, enable, encourage, facilitate, authorise, support
📝 The court formally **interdicted** any further sale or transfer of the disputed land.

**INTOLERANT** *(adjective)* | অসহিষ্ণু, পক্ষপাতদুষ্ট
Not willing to accept views, beliefs, or behaviour that differ from one's own.
✅ narrow-minded, bigoted, prejudiced, inflexible, closed-minded, rigid
❌ tolerant, open-minded, accepting, flexible, understanding, broad-minded
📝 He is deeply **intolerant** of even the mildest and most constructive criticism.

**INTRIGUING** *(adjective)* | কৌতূহলোদ্দীপক, রহস্যময়
Arousing curiosity or interest in a mysterious way; fascinating and complex.
✅ fascinating, interesting, curious, mysterious, captivating, engaging
❌ boring, dull, uninteresting, tedious, predictable, tiresome, bland
📝 The detective novel had a genuinely **intriguing** and cleverly constructed mystery at its heart.

**INTUITIVE** *(adjective)* | সহজাত, স্বজ্ঞাত
Using or based on what one feels is true even without conscious reasoning.
✅ instinctive, natural, innate, spontaneous, gut-driven, perceptive
❌ logical, reasoned, deliberate, calculated, analytical, rational, studied
📝 She has an almost **intuitive** sense of exactly what her customers want and need.

**INVISIBLE** *(adjective)* | অদৃশ্য, দেখা যায় না
Unable to be seen; not visible to the eye; not noticed or perceived.
✅ unseen, hidden, imperceptible, undetectable, concealed, out of sight
❌ visible, apparent, obvious, noticeable, clear, evident, conspicuous
📝 Germs and bacteria are completely **invisible** to the naked and unaided human eye.

## 🔤 L–O (New)

**LAPSE** *(noun/verb)* | ভুল, স্খলন; ক্রমে পড়ে যাওয়া
A temporary failure; a slight error; to fall gradually into an inferior state.
✅ failure, slip, error, mistake, fault, decline, drop, deterioration
❌ success, improvement, achievement, progress, advancement, correction
📝 A single minor **lapse** in concentration was enough to cause the costly accident.

**LIBERTY** *(noun)* | স্বাধীনতা, মুক্তি
The state of being free within society from oppressive restrictions; freedom.
✅ freedom, independence, autonomy, rights, emancipation, self-determination
❌ slavery, imprisonment, oppression, captivity, bondage, subjugation
📝 Generations of brave freedom fighters sacrificed everything they had for **liberty**.

**LURE** *(verb/noun)* | প্রলুব্ধ করা, টোপ দেওয়া
To tempt someone to do something or go somewhere; a bait or temptation.
✅ tempt, entice, attract, seduce, draw, coax, bait, persuade
❌ repel, deter, warn, discourage, push away, repulse, drive away
📝 The incredibly generous offer **lured** dozens of talented investors into the project.

**MALEFICENT** *(adjective)* | ক্ষতিকর, অশুভ, ক্ষতিসাধনকারী
Causing harm or destruction; actively evil or wicked in its effects.
✅ harmful, evil, wicked, destructive, malicious, sinister, baleful
❌ beneficial, good, kind, helpful, harmless, benevolent, constructive
📝 In the fairy tale, the witch was portrayed as a deeply powerful and **maleficent** figure.

**MASSIVE** *(adjective)* | বিশাল, বিপুল
Large and heavy or solid; exceptionally large in scale or degree; enormous.
✅ huge, enormous, immense, gigantic, colossal, vast, substantial, great
❌ tiny, small, slight, little, minor, petite, lightweight, insignificant
📝 The company suffered a truly **massive** and unprecedented financial loss last quarter.

**METTLE** *(noun)* | সাহস, মনোবল, দৃঢ়তা
A person's ability to cope well with difficulties; spirit and resilience.
✅ courage, spirit, bravery, determination, resilience, fortitude, grit
❌ cowardice, weakness, timidity, frailty, fear, spinelessness, fragility
📝 The crisis truly tested the young leader's **mettle** and she proved more than equal to it.

**MORBID** *(adjective)* | বিষণ্ণ, রুগ্ণ, মৃত্যু বিষয়ে আসক্ত
Having or expressing an abnormal and unhealthy interest in death or disease.
✅ gloomy, ghastly, macabre, dark, gruesome, sombre, grim, unhealthy
❌ cheerful, healthy, positive, lively, light-hearted, pleasant, bright
📝 He has always had a strange and **morbid** fascination with death and dark literature.

**NAIVE** *(adjective)* | সরল, অনভিজ্ঞ, বোকাসোকা
Showing a lack of experience, wisdom, or judgment; simple and innocent.
✅ innocent, simple, gullible, unsophisticated, inexperienced, credulous
❌ experienced, worldly, sophisticated, shrewd, cynical, knowing, wise
📝 He was far too **naive** to realise that the complete stranger was actively deceiving him.

## 📚 BATCH — 30 Missing Words from Hindi Transcript Session

**AD HOC** *(adjective/adverb)* | বিশেষ উদ্দেশ্যে তৈরি, তাৎক্ষণিক
Created or done for a particular purpose only; not planned in advance.
✅ improvised, unplanned, makeshift, temporary, spontaneous, purpose-specific
❌ permanent, planned, systematic, organised, pre-arranged, regular
📝 The committee formed an **ad hoc** group to deal with the unexpected crisis.

**AMBIDEXTROUS** *(adjective)* | উভয় হাতে সমান দক্ষ
Able to use the right and left hand equally well; versatile and skillful.
✅ two-handed, versatile, skilled with both hands, adaptable, dexterous
❌ one-handed, clumsy, unskilled, inept, limited
📝 The **ambidextrous** artist could paint with both hands simultaneously without difficulty.

**BITTERNESS** *(noun)* | কড়াপন, তিক্ততা, মনোকষ্ট
Anger and disappointment at being treated unfairly; a sharp unpleasant taste.
✅ resentment, acrimony, rancor, hostility, animosity, harshness, acerbity
❌ sweetness, goodwill, friendliness, warmth, contentment, forgiveness
📝 Years of unfair treatment left deep **bitterness** in his heart.

**CONTENTMENT** *(noun)* | তৃপ্তি, সন্তুষ্টি
A state of happiness and satisfaction; peaceful ease and fulfilment.
✅ satisfaction, happiness, peace, fulfilment, ease, serenity, pleasure
❌ dissatisfaction, discontent, misery, restlessness, unhappiness, yearning
📝 She found genuine **contentment** in simple daily routines and her garden.

**CYNICAL** *(adjective)* | সন্দেহপ্রবণ, মানুষের ভালো উদ্দেশ্যে অবিশ্বাসী
Believing that people are motivated only by self-interest; distrustful of others.
✅ skeptical, distrustful, suspicious, pessimistic, sarcastic, doubtful, sardonic
❌ trusting, optimistic, idealistic, naive, hopeful, believing, positive
📝 Years of disappointment had made him deeply **cynical** about politics.

**ECSTATIC** *(adjective)* | আনন্দে উচ্ছ্বসিত, অতি উৎফুল্ল
Feeling or expressing overwhelming happiness or joyful excitement.
✅ overjoyed, elated, jubilant, thrilled, euphoric, exhilarated, rapturous
❌ miserable, depressed, gloomy, sad, dejected, despondent, downcast
📝 The fans were **ecstatic** when their team scored the winning goal.

**ELATION** *(noun)* | উৎফুল্লতা, পরম আনন্দ
Great happiness and exhilaration; a feeling of triumphant joy.
✅ joy, euphoria, jubilation, delight, exhilaration, happiness, thrill
❌ depression, misery, sadness, despair, gloom, despondency, sorrow
📝 She could barely contain her **elation** after receiving the acceptance letter.

**ENTHUSIASM** *(noun)* | উৎসাহ, উদ্দীপনা
Intense and eager enjoyment, interest, or approval; energetic keenness.
✅ eagerness, zeal, passion, keenness, fervor, excitement, energy, ardor
❌ apathy, indifference, lethargy, boredom, disinterest, reluctance
📝 Her **enthusiasm** for the project inspired the whole team to work harder.

**ENVY** *(noun/verb)* | ঈর্ষা, হিংসা
A feeling of discontented longing for someone else's possessions or qualities.
✅ jealousy, resentment, covetousness, desire, longing, begrudging
❌ contentment, generosity, goodwill, satisfaction, happiness for others
📝 He tried to hide his **envy** when his colleague received the promotion.

**FELICITY** *(noun)* | সুখ, আনন্দ, সৌভাগ্য
Intense happiness; the ability to find appropriate expression for something.
✅ happiness, bliss, joy, delight, blessedness, contentment, pleasure
❌ misery, unhappiness, sorrow, sadness, grief, wretchedness
📝 The poet described the **felicity** of childhood as something lost forever.

**FLAMBOYANT** *(adjective)* | জমকালো, চটকদার, অতি উৎসাহী
Tending to attract attention because of exuberance, confidence, or style.
✅ showy, extravagant, ostentatious, flashy, vivid, theatrical, bold, dazzling
❌ modest, reserved, understated, quiet, plain, restrained, simple
📝 His **flamboyant** personality lit up every room he entered.

**FLUSTERED** *(adjective)* | ঘাবড়ানো, উত্তেজিত ও বিচলিত
Agitated, confused, and nervous; upset and flustered by a sudden difficulty.
✅ agitated, confused, flustered, nervous, rattled, unsettled, disconcerted
❌ calm, composed, relaxed, unruffled, collected, self-assured, steady
📝 She became completely **flustered** when the interviewer asked an unexpected question.

**GRUMPY** *(adjective)* | খিটখিটে, বিরক্ত, রুষ্ট
Bad-tempered and sulky; irritably dissatisfied and discontented.
✅ irritable, cranky, bad-tempered, grouchy, sulky, crotchety, peevish
❌ cheerful, pleasant, good-natured, happy, amiable, content, sunny
📝 He was always **grumpy** in the morning before his first cup of tea.

**INCOGNITO** *(adjective/adverb)* | ছদ্মবেশে, পরিচয় গোপন করে
Having one's true identity concealed; in disguise or under a false name.
✅ disguised, anonymous, unidentified, undercover, in disguise, unknown
❌ known, identified, revealed, recognised, uncovered, open, public
📝 The celebrity travelled **incognito** to avoid being recognised by fans.

**INFURIATED** *(adjective)* | অত্যন্ত রাগান্বিত, ক্রুদ্ধ
Extremely angry; provoked to fury; made furious.
✅ enraged, furious, livid, incensed, irate, outraged, seething, angered
❌ calm, placid, pleased, satisfied, content, composed, unruffled
📝 She was **infuriated** by his repeated failure to keep his promises.

**ISOLATION** *(noun)* | বিচ্ছিন্নতা, একাকীত্ব
The process or fact of being isolated; the state of being alone or apart.
✅ solitude, seclusion, separation, aloneness, detachment, quarantine, loneliness
❌ connection, integration, togetherness, inclusion, community, belonging
📝 Prolonged **isolation** during the lockdown affected many people's mental health.

**LETHARGY** *(noun)* | আলস্য, নিস্তেজ ভাব, অবসাদ
A lack of energy and enthusiasm; physical or mental sluggishness.
✅ sluggishness, inactivity, drowsiness, laziness, fatigue, torpor, listlessness
❌ energy, vitality, enthusiasm, vigour, alertness, liveliness, dynamism
📝 A poor diet can cause **lethargy** and difficulty concentrating throughout the day.

**MELLIFLUOUS** *(adjective)* | মধুর, সুরেলা, কানে মিষ্টি লাগে এমন
Sweet or musical; pleasant to hear; flowing smoothly and sweetly.
✅ sweet-sounding, melodious, musical, harmonious, tuneful, dulcet, smooth
❌ harsh, grating, discordant, unpleasant, jarring, rasping, rough
📝 Her **mellifluous** voice made even the most difficult poem sound beautiful.

**MISERY** *(noun)* | দুঃখ, কষ্ট, দুর্দশা
A state of great suffering, distress, or discomfort; wretchedness.
✅ suffering, distress, wretchedness, hardship, anguish, unhappiness, pain
❌ happiness, joy, comfort, pleasure, contentment, ease, delight
📝 The refugees endured months of **misery** before reaching safety.

**PARANOID** *(adjective)* | অযৌক্তিক সন্দেহপ্রবণ, প্যারানয়েড
Unreasonably anxious or fearful that others are trying to harm one.
✅ suspicious, distrustful, fearful, anxious, irrational, delusional, obsessive
❌ trusting, calm, rational, secure, confident, relaxed, sane
📝 After the theft, he became increasingly **paranoid** about his home security.

**PERPLEXED** *(adjective)* | হতবুদ্ধি, বিভ্রান্ত, ধাঁধায় পড়া
Completely baffled; very puzzled and unable to understand something.
✅ puzzled, baffled, confused, bewildered, mystified, stumped, at a loss
❌ clear, certain, confident, understanding, enlightened, sure, composed
📝 The detective looked **perplexed** as the evidence seemed to point in opposite directions.

**PERSISTENCE** *(noun)* | অধ্যবসায়, দৃঢ়তা, হাল না ছাড়া
The fact of continuing in a course of action despite difficulty; tenacity.
✅ tenacity, determination, perseverance, stubbornness, endurance, resolve
❌ giving up, surrendering, capitulation, quitting, indecision, weakness
📝 Her **persistence** through years of rejection finally led to her breakthrough.

**PROLIX** *(adjective)* | বাচাল, দীর্ঘ ও বিরক্তিকর বক্তব্যের
Using too many words; tediously lengthy; wordy and excessively long.
✅ verbose, long-winded, wordy, rambling, garrulous, tedious, overlong
❌ concise, brief, succinct, terse, laconic, pithy, to-the-point
📝 His **prolix** speeches tested the patience of even his most devoted supporters.

**RANCID** *(adjective)* | বাসি, পচা গন্ধওয়ালা
(Of food) smelling or tasting unpleasant as a result of being stale or spoiled.
✅ spoiled, stale, foul, putrid, sour, tainted, off, rank
❌ fresh, wholesome, sweet, pure, good, clean, pleasant-smelling
📝 The **rancid** butter had been left outside in the heat too long.

**RESISTANCE** *(noun)* | প্রতিরোধ, বাধা, রোধশক্তি
The refusal to accept or comply with something; the ability to withstand.
✅ opposition, defiance, refusal, obstruction, struggle, fight, resilience
❌ compliance, acceptance, submission, surrender, yielding, cooperation
📝 There was strong community **resistance** to the construction of the new highway.

**SANGUINE** *(adjective)* | আশাবাদী, প্রফুল্ল, উৎসাহী
Optimistic or positive, especially in a difficult situation; cheerfully hopeful.
✅ optimistic, hopeful, positive, confident, cheerful, upbeat, buoyant
❌ pessimistic, gloomy, despondent, hopeless, negative, cynical, fearful
📝 Despite the setbacks, she remained **sanguine** about the project's eventual success.

**SOLITARY** *(adjective)* | একাকী, নিঃসঙ্গ
Done or existing alone; without companions; seeking or preferring solitude.
✅ alone, isolated, lonely, reclusive, secluded, single, lone, hermit-like
❌ sociable, gregarious, accompanied, together, communal, friendly, outgoing
📝 He lived a **solitary** life in a small cottage by the mountains.

**SOLITUDE** *(noun)* | একাকিত্ব, নির্জনতা
The state of being alone, especially when this is peaceful and pleasant.
✅ seclusion, isolation, aloneness, quiet, peace, retreat, privacy
❌ company, togetherness, community, sociability, crowd, companionship
📝 The writer sought **solitude** in a remote cabin to finish his novel.

**SWINDLER** *(noun)* | প্রতারক, ঠগ, জালিয়াতি করে এমন ব্যক্তি
A person who swindles others; a cheat or fraudster.
✅ fraudster, cheat, deceiver, con artist, trickster, crook, fraudster, impostor
❌ honest person, trustworthy person, benefactor, upright citizen
📝 The **swindler** posed as a legitimate investor to steal from pensioners.

**WRETCHED** *(adjective)* | দুর্দশাগ্রস্ত, হতভাগ্য, নিকৃষ্ট
In a very unhappy or unfortunate state; of poor quality; very bad.
✅ miserable, unhappy, pitiable, pathetic, deplorable, poor, awful, woeful
❌ happy, fortunate, excellent, comfortable, good, splendid, blessed
📝 The refugees lived in **wretched** conditions with little food or shelter.
